## THAILAND FOCUS TREE
## Five political paths from the 2000 start: Thaksinist Populism (A), Royalist
## Establishment (B), Democrat Establishment (E), Reform Wave (C, a 2020-protest-era
## entry), and Greater Thailand irredentism (D, off the military branch), plus a
## Communist entry (Ghosts of the Jungle) and a shared economic/diplomatic trunk.
focus_tree = {
	id = thailand_focus

	country = {
		factor = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			tag = SIA
		}
	}

	# Focus Shortcuts
	# Each shortcut is a quick-jump button: target = the focus id it scrolls to.
	# name is a unique label; scroll_wheel_factor matches the MD convention.
	shortcut = {
		name = SIA_lost_territories_shortcut
		target = SIA_burma_breakaway_question

			country_event = thailand.50
			506 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = rubber
					amount = 10
				}
			}
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = SIA_recover_97_util
				bonus = 1.0
				uses = 1
				category = CAT_util
			}
			# Recovery spine Step 1: float the baht. Capital flight and the export
			# collapse end (trade/min_export clear) and the revenue base steadies,
			# but the IMF loan arrives with interest and austerity attached.
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_crisis_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_trade_opinion_factor = 0.25 tooltip = trade_opinion_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_min_export = 0.08 tooltip = min_export_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_interest_rate_multiplier_modifier = -1 tooltip = interest_rate_multiplier_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_consumer_goods_factor = -0.04 tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = tax_gain_multiplier_modifier_tt }
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_economy_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_min_export = 0.05 tooltip = min_export_tt }

			clr_country_flag = SIA_imf_program_active
			# Also granted by the SIA_tax_modernization decision - guard so the two
			# sources never stack the same bonus twice.
			# Recovery spine Step 4: the early IMF payoff ends austerity, so the consumer
			# goods burden and the revenue drag lift together; the loan interest only
			# eases partway (the stability dip is the IMF crutch leaving with the program).

	focus = {
		id = SIA_seeds_of_reform
		icon = liberalization

		x = 21
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_2001_election

		cost = 5

		# Restructure: demoted out of the 2001 fork. The reform wave is a
		# later-era movement that grows out of the 2020 street protests, positioned
		# below "The Youth in the Streets" as the prerequisite of its own downstream
		# focuses (layout R2 still holds). No date gate: it opens the moment Future
		# Forward (socialism, idx 3) crosses a 5% vote share, mirroring the Communist
		# ghosts_of_the_jungle gate. Completing it hands idx 3 a decisive popularity
		# swing so the movement can WIN power; "A Future Forward" and onward only open
		# once idx 3 actually rules (SIA_reform_trunk_open).
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = SIA_reform_5pct_tt
				check_variable = { party_pop_array^3 > 0.05 }
			}
		}
		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_2001_election }
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_seeds_of_reform"
			# This is the CAMPAIGN, not the trunk switch. Do NOT set SIA_path_reform
			# or clear the other path flags here. Doing so would strand a player still
			# committed to another trunk (their downstream locks while idx 3 does not
			# yet rule), and pre-setting the flag suppresses the realignment detector's
			# thailand.422 -> SIA_realign_to_reform when idx 3 actually wins (which is
			# what clears the old flags and strips the outgoing party's ideas). The
			# trunk switch happens on the win, not here.
			# Decisive campaign surge: hand Future Forward (idx 3) a big popularity
			# and outlook boost so it can win the next election and unlock its trunk.
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.25 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.25 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_outlook_western_tt

			recalculate_party = yes
			add_timed_idea = { idea = SIA_reform_spring days = 365 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -15 }
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 35
		}
	}

				set_variable = { SIA_ds_grievance = 40 }
				SIA_ds_recompute_floor = yes

			}

			country_event = { id = thailand.350 days = 1 }

	# HOMELAND DEFENSE — Fortifications & territorial defence
	# Thematically a sub-branch of SIA_armed_forces_modernization, but placed right
	# of the Kra Gateway canvas (x=15 rel SIA_kra_gateway) like the economy-expansion
	# roots. Gated on AFM via `available` (has_completed_focus), NOT a prerequisite —
	# draws no cross-zone line (layout_audit R3 whitelist). Head grants baseline
	# infantry defence + land-fort construction speed; the capability pillars (air
	# defence / land forts / anti-amphibious) and the regional fortify focuses
	# (north/east/south) chain below via relative_position_id to their prerequisite.
	########################

	focus = {
		id = SIA_territorial_defense
		icon = military_sphere

		x = -8
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_ministry_of_defence
		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_ministry_of_defence }

		cost = 8

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_LAWS FOCUS_FILTER_EXPENDITURE }

		available = {
			has_completed_focus = SIA_armed_forces_modernization
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_territorial_defense"
			army_experience = 25
			add_command_power = 30
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_army_infantry_defence_factor = 0.05 tooltip = army_infantry_defence_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_production_speed_bunker_factor = 0.10 tooltip = production_speed_bunker_factor_tt }
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	########################
	# WAVE 5 — THE FLAME REKINDLED (Communist revival path, ahistorical)
	# Position: spine at x=41 y=5 running south to y=12.
	# Space: Kra Canal occupies x=37–39; Branch D at x=47. Corridor x=40–46 is free,
	#   so the spine (x=41) carries side-forks at x=39 (left) and x=43 (right).
	# Entry gate: misery gate (SIA_misery_gate flag, ≥52 consecutive weeks stability<35%),
	#   OR 2020 crackdown + household debt path, OR republic declared + stability<40%.
	# Strategic forks (give the path real choices, not a flat spine):
	#   1. Road to power — SIA_general_strike (urban) MX SIA_protracted_peoples_war (rural).
	#   2. Consolidation — SIA_peoples_tribunals (terror) MX SIA_broad_united_front (coalition).
	#   3. Development   — SIA_peoples_army (guns) MX SIA_socialist_education (schools).
	#   4. Foreign road  — SIA_the_beijing_road (China's orbit) MX SIA_indochina_federation.
	# AI: base 0 with ahistorical escape hatch (matches Branch D pattern).
	########################

	focus = {
		id = SIA_ghosts_of_the_jungle
		icon = political_pressure_dark_green

		x = 28
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_2001_election

		cost = 6

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_2001_election }

		available = {
			# The party only marches once it has a real base — the Communist Party
			# (sub-ideology idx 4) must hold at least 5% of the popular vote.
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = SIA_ghosts_5pct_tt
				check_variable = { party_pop_array^4 > 0.05 }
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_ghosts_of_the_jungle"
			set_country_flag = SIA_path_communist
			# Grow the emerging (communist) outlook alongside the party share, or the
			# relative shift just caps out at Thailand's near-zero communist bloc.
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.20 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.20 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_outlook_emerging_tt
			recalculate_party = yes
			add_ideas = SIA_red_revival
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 3
				is_historical_focus_on = no
				check_variable = { party_pop_array^4 > 0.05 }
			}
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = SIA_isaan_soviets
		icon = farming

		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_ghosts_of_the_jungle

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_ghosts_of_the_jungle }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_ECONOMY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_isaan_soviets"
			# Raise the emerging (communist) outlook too, else the party share caps out.
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.15 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.15 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_outlook_emerging_tt
			recalculate_party = yes
			set_country_flag = SIA_village_committees_unlocked
			# N-1: SIA_western_sanctions can also arrive via thailand.251.a;
			# guard so this grant is idempotent rather than a silent no-op double-add.
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SIA_western_sanctions } } add_ideas = SIA_western_sanctions }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = USA } USA = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = SIA modifier = medium_decrease } } }
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = SIA_general_strike
		icon = political_pressure

		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_isaan_soviets

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_isaan_soviets }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = SIA_protracted_peoples_war }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_general_strike"
			custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_general_strike_tt
			# Urban mass mobilization pays out in political muscle up front; the
			# rural road (Protracted People's War) buys military strength instead.
			# Lift the emerging (communist) outlook too, else the party share caps out.
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.08 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.08 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_outlook_emerging_tt
			recalculate_party = yes
			add_political_power = 50
			# Open the 180-day strike: a decision-driven push toward capitulation.
			# The SIA_general_strike_category decisions raise SIA_strike_momentum
			# (0-100); hit 100 and the government blinks (thailand.250, peaceful road).
			# The weekly on_action bleeds -1% stability while it runs. If the clock
			# runs out below 100 the generals crush it (thailand.259, junta coup).
			set_country_flag = SIA_strike_active
			set_variable = { SIA_strike_momentum = 0 }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = SIA_general_strike_idea days = 180 }
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = thailand.259 days = 180 }
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
		}
	}

	# Fork 1 (left) — the rural road. Protracted people's war instead of the urban
	# general strike. Mutually exclusive with SIA_general_strike; either road reaches
	# SIA_people_seize_power (OR prerequisite below).
	focus = {
		id = SIA_protracted_peoples_war
		icon = focus_CHI_guerrilla_warfare

		x = 2
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_isaan_soviets

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_isaan_soviets }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = SIA_general_strike }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_LAWS }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_protracted_peoples_war"
			# This is the armed road — set the same flag the crackdown branch of
			# thailand.250 sets, so downstream content reads one consistent state.
			set_country_flag = SIA_communist_armed_path
			# Lift the emerging (communist) outlook too, else the party share caps out.
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.10 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.10 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_outlook_emerging_tt
			recalculate_party = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_army_morale_factor = 0.05 tooltip = army_morale_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_experience_gain_army_factor = 0.1 tooltip = experience_gain_army_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_army_org_factor = 0.05 tooltip = army_org_factor_tt }
			army_experience = 25
			hidden_effect = {
				news_event = { id = thailand_news.31 days = 2 }
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = SIA_people_seize_power
		icon = armed_revolution

		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_general_strike

		cost = 5

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_general_strike focus = SIA_protracted_peoples_war }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

		available = {
			# You cannot simply click your way into power. Either the general strike has
			# forced the government to capitulate (the peaceful road, which auto-completes
			# this focus from thailand.250), or you have committed to the armed struggle.
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = SIA_seize_power_gate_tt
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = SIA_communist_peaceful_path
					has_country_flag = SIA_communist_armed_path
				}
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_people_seize_power"
			# Remove old-regime ideas if held (both roads)
			SIA_remove_barami = yes
			SIA_remove_three_pillars = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = SIA_network_monarchy_idea }
				remove_ideas = SIA_network_monarchy_idea
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = SIA_kings_moral_authority }
				remove_ideas = SIA_kings_moral_authority
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = SIA_peoples_constitution }
				remove_ideas = SIA_peoples_constitution
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = SIA_junta_government }
				remove_ideas = SIA_junta_government
			}
			# The armed road takes power by force: the deposed establishment does not
			# concede, it fights. Split the country BEFORE converting our own government
			# so the old regime breaks away as its own nationalist and royalist rebels
			# rather than a mirror of the new red state. ruling_party = communism keeps
			# us (the loyalists) as the communist bloc, while ideology = nationalist arms
			# the deposed royalist and military establishment against us.
			# The peaceful road (general strike capitulation) skips the war entirely, the
			# establishment there conceded rather than fight.
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = SIA_communist_armed_path }
				start_civil_war = {
					ruling_party = communism
					ideology = nationalist
					size = 0.45
				}
				add_war_support = 0.10
			}
			# Formalize the communist state on our side: exact party index, name, leader
			# and suspended elections (thailand.70-style protocol). On the armed road this
			# runs after the split, converting only the loyalist half; on the peaceful
			# road it is the whole bloodless takeover.
			set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
			change_ruling_party_effect = yes
			update_party_name = yes
			set_leader_SIA = yes
			# World reactions fired from the seize-power event
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = thailand.251 days = 3 }
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
		}
	}

	# Fork 2 — consolidating the revolution. How does the new state treat the old
	# order and its own enemies? Optional MX pair (dead-end capability picks).
	focus = {
		id = SIA_peoples_tribunals
		icon = communist_purge

		x = 4
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_people_seize_power

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_people_seize_power }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = SIA_broad_united_front }

		available = { SIA_communist_trunk_open = yes }
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_peoples_tribunals"
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_politics_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_pol_drift_defence_factor = 0.15 tooltip = drift_defence_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_pol_political_power_factor = 0.05 tooltip = political_power_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_pol_stability_factor = -0.03 tooltip = stability_factor_tt }
			# Revolutionary security organs crush the active insurgency, if any.
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = SIA_ds_active }
				custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_insurgency_intensity_decrease_tt
				subtract_from_variable = { SIA_insurgency_intensity = 5 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SIA_insurgency_intensity min = 0 max = 100 }
				SIA_ds_update_tier = yes
			}

	# ─── v0.9 Develop conquered land — ASEAN hegemony payoff ───
	# The claim focuses stake the whole region but end at wargoals. This capstone
	# turns a conqueror's map into an empire that holds: it opens the reconstruction
	# decisions (pacify -> develop -> core) over every seized province and grants the
	# occupation apparatus, so the New Order can actually be administered rather than
	# just declared. Reachable from either the maritime or the mainland claim branch.
	focus = {
		id = SIA_the_new_order
		icon = irredentism

		x = 0
		y = 3
		relative_position_id = SIA_asean_hegemony

		cost = 7

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_maritime_claims focus = SIA_mainland_claims }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_the_new_order"
			set_country_flag = SIA_reconstruction_unlocked
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SIA_occupation_administration } }
				add_ideas = SIA_occupation_administration
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { temp_productivity_change = -30 }
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { SIA_is_reclaimed_state = yes }
				add_compliance = 10
				state_flat_productivity_change_effect = yes
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
		}
	}

	# B6 - ROYAL & SUFFICIENCY ("Sufficiency and the Crown")
	# Positioned right of the Kra Gateway canvas (thailand-economy-expansion-spec.md
	# §4 B6); the head is a root off SIA_kra_gateway, same as the ECONOMIC EXPANSION
	# BRANCH above (:11884). Intended chain:
	#   SIA_sufficiency_and_the_crown (head)
	#     -> SIA_new_theory_agriculture / SIA_doi_tung_highlands / SIA_chaipattana_water
	#        (3 parallel royal projects) + SIA_crown_property_portfolio
	#     -> ME fork: SIA_deepen_sufficiency | SIA_pragmatic_balance (needs the 3 projects)
	#     -> SIA_resilient_kingdom (capstone, needs either fork end)
	# All 8 focuses sit at relative_position_id = SIA_sufficiency_and_the_crown; every
	# focus-completion gate is now wired as a real `prerequisite` (converted from
	# `available`/has_completed_focus).
	########################

	focus = {
		id = SIA_sufficiency_and_the_crown
		icon = goal_generic_support_monarchy

		x = 9
		y = 0
		relative_position_id = SIA_kra_gateway
		available = {
			has_completed_focus = SIA_recover_from_97_crisis
			# Sufficiency-and-Crown is a monarchist economic doctrine: a communist or
			# a Move Forward (socialism, ruling_party idx 3) government will not run it.
			# Wrapped so the array check still gives the player a reason (no auto-tooltip).
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = SIA_sufficiency_gov_gate_tt
				NOT = { has_government = communism }
				NOT = { is_in_array = { ruling_party = 3 } }
			}
		}

		cost = 5

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY FOCUS_FILTER_ECONOMY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_sufficiency_and_the_crown"
			add_ideas = SIA_sufficiency_resilience

			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_economy_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_expected_welfare_modifier = -0.25 tooltip = expected_welfare_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_productivity_growth_modifier = -0.05 tooltip = productivity_growth_modifier_tt }

			# Sacrifice: turning inward toward self-reliance forgoes the dynamism of the growth-maximising path (unconditional cost - the guarded farmer/conglomerate swings below only fire if Thailand has those groups).
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = farmers }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
				change_farmers_opinion = yes
			}
			# Flavour: sufficiency rhetoric alienates the big conglomerates who profit from growth-maximisation (fires only if Thailand has gained the industrial_conglomerates group).
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = industrial_conglomerates }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
				change_industrial_conglomerates_opinion = yes
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	########################
	# B2 - TOURISM ("Amazing Thailand")
	# Positioned right of the Kra Gateway canvas (thailand-economy-expansion-spec.md
	# §4 B2); the head is a root off SIA_kra_gateway, same as the ECONOMIC EXPANSION
	# BRANCH above (:11884) and B6 (:12491). Intended chain:
	#   SIA_tourism_masterplan (head)
	#     -> SIA_phuket_andaman / SIA_pattaya_eastern_riviera / SIA_chiang_mai_lanna
	#        (3 convergent destinations) + SIA_medical_wellness_hub (all 4 gated on the head)
	#     -> SIA_rebuild_the_andaman (off SIA_phuket_andaman, post-tsunami rebuild)
	#     -> ME fork: SIA_volume_tourism | SIA_high_value_tourism (needs the 3 destinations)
	#     -> SIA_tourism_capital (capstone, needs either fork end)

	# B4 - FINANCE & CAPITAL MARKETS ("The Baht and the Bourse")
	# Positioned right of the Kra Gateway canvas (thailand-economy-expansion-spec.md

	# BRANCH above (:11884), B6 (:12491), and B2 (:12828). Intended chain:
	#   SIA_baht_and_bourse (head)
	#     -> SIA_tamc_workout (gated on the head)
	#     -> ME fork: SIA_consumer_credit_boom | SIA_responsible_lending (gated on the head)
	#     -> SIA_deepen_the_set / SIA_vayupak_stabilization / SIA_bank_consolidation
	#        (3 capital-markets projects, gated on the ME fork being resolved)
	#     -> SIA_financial_center (capstone, needs all 3 capital-markets projects)
	# All 8 focuses sit at relative_position_id = SIA_baht_and_bourse; the head
	# itself sits at relative_position_id = SIA_clean_the_banks; every focus-completion
	# gate is now wired as a real `prerequisite` (converted from `available`/
	# has_completed_focus).
	# Interlocks the pre-existing finance singles: SIA_clean_the_banks (:227, the
	# 1997-crisis bad-bank founding - SIA_tamc_workout is the same institution's
	# final wind-down decades later, not a duplicate), SIA_virtual_banks (:12271,
	# referenced by the capstone's date-gated digital-banking bonus),
	# SIA_capital_of_capital (:12390), and SIA_make_thailand_investor (:12419).
	# No household-debt meter: the credit cycle here is consumer_goods/stability/
	# tax_gain/expected_welfare, and loc says "consumer credit," never "debt."

	########################
	# B1 - OUTWARD EXPANSION ("The Outward Turn")
	# Positioned right of the Kra Gateway canvas (thailand-economy-expansion-spec.md
	# §4 B1) - the marquee "conglomerate goes global" branch, appended last after
	# B6 (:12491), B2 (:12828), B4 (:13185), B5 (:13515), and B3 (:13870); the head
	# is a root off SIA_kra_gateway, same house convention. Intended chain:
	#   SIA_the_outward_turn (head)
	#     -> ME fork: SIA_leveraged_expansion | SIA_organic_expansion (financing approach, gated on the head)
	#     -> SIA_cp_into_china / SIA_thaibev_conquers_asean / SIA_scg_long_son /
	#        SIA_central_minor_abroad / SIA_ptt_banpu_resources
	#        (5 parallel outbound deals, gated on the ME fork being resolved)
	#     -> SIA_outbound_statecraft (capstone, needs any 3 of the 5 deals)
	# All 9 focuses sit at relative_position_id = SIA_the_outward_turn; the head
	# itself sits at relative_position_id = SIA_industrial_estates; every focus-completion
	# gate is now wired as a real `prerequisite` (converted from `available`/
	# has_completed_focus).
	# Any-3-of-5 capstone gate: C(5,3) = 10 combinations, spelled out as a 10-way
	# OR of AND-triples (no new counter variable) - the same pattern B5's
	# any-2-of-3 gate uses at 3 combinations (SIA_resource_sovereignty, :13824).
	# Country tags verified against common/country_tags/00_countries.txt: Cambodia
	# = CBD, China = CHI, Vietnam = VIE (not VNM/VTN), Singapore = SIN.
	# Deviation from spec §4 B1: the spec table's SIA_thaibev_conquers_asean row
	# lists a "▼the_ulema" swing, but the_ulema is AA_law_internal_factions.txt's
	# Muslim-internal-faction mechanic (its own opinion/variable system granted
	# only to countries with that internal-faction setup), not one of the guarded
	# interest groups this branch's pattern uses - and Thailand never has that
	# idea. Dropped; the deal's real cost is the treasury outlay below instead.
	# Writedown risk (leveraged path only): SIA_scg_long_son (LSP),
	# SIA_central_minor_abroad (Signa), and SIA_ptt_banpu_resources (KKD) each
	# carry a `# TODO thailand.EX4/EX5/EX6` comment for a future writedown event
	# gated on has_country_flag = SIA_outbound_leveraged (set by
	# SIA_leveraged_expansion) - no country_event call is added here (spec §4 B1,
	# §8, deferred to the events pass). The leveraged path's own
	# `-stability_factor` at the fork is what keeps it honestly costed before
	# those events land (B5 precedent, :13618).
	# Interlocks: SIA_central_minor_abroad -> B2 tourism (SIA_tourism_masterplan,
	# :12857, same hospitality/retail playbook run abroad); SIA_ptt_banpu_resources
	# -> B5 resources (SIA_the_ground_beneath, :13541, same resource-sector
	# playbook run abroad instead of under Thai soil).
	########################

	# B7 - INTELLIGENCE & POLICING (spec §4b thailand-economy-expansion-spec.md).
	# SECURITY branch, not economy - kind = political/security, own PR/review pass.
	# Head SIA_the_national_intelligence (founds the NIA) -> two parallel arms
	# (Domestic Policing: special_branch_police/counter_insurgency_south ‖ Foreign
	# Intelligence: signals_intelligence/humint_networks) -> ME foreign-partner
	# fork (cia_partnership vs mss_cooperation, both La Resistance-gated) ->
	# SIA_the_watchful_state (needs at least one focus from each arm) -> ME
	# capstone sacrifice fork (surveillance_state "Panopticon" vs
	# accountable_oversight "Rule of Law"). All 10 focuses sit at
	# relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence; the head itself sits at
	# relative_position_id = SIA_armed_forces_modernization (x=19, y=1) after the
	# maintainer's manual re-wiring. Every focus-completion gate is a real
	# `prerequisite`.
	#
	# DLC gating (hide-completely, not graceful-fallback): the whole intelligence-
	# agency spine is La Resistance-only. The seven agency-effect focuses
	# (signals_intelligence, humint_networks, cia_partnership, mss_cooperation,
	# the_watchful_state, surveillance_state, accountable_oversight) carry
	# allow_branch = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" } and are hidden entirely without
	# the DLC. The head and the two policing focuses (special_branch_police,
	# counter_insurgency_south) stay universal: the head still creates the agency
	# only under DLC (else a PP fallback), and counter_insurgency_south keeps its
	# own if-has_dlc/else. A non-DLC player therefore sees a compact 3-focus
	# policing stub; a DLC player sees the full branch.
	#
	# Interest groups: Thailand's roster is only the_military/landowners/
	# small_medium_business_owners (spec input, verified against common/ideas/
	# AA_law_internal_factions.txt) - the_military is the one live swing this
	# branch uses (guarded ▲ on special_branch_police). intelligence_community
	# opinion swings are guarded no-ops today (Thailand never holds that idea) but
	# kept as future-proof texture per the spec's faction-lever pattern. Every
	# focus still carries a real NON-faction cost (treasury, stability, PP,
	# expected_police, foreign_influence_defense, or a lower detection ceiling) so
	# no reward is pure upside (spec §3 sacrifice principle).
	#
	# Reuses the existing SIA_military_modifier/SIA_politics_modifier accumulators
	# (99_SIA_dynamic_modifiers.txt) rather than a new B7-only counter - GISTDA
	# (:790) and the anti-narcotics bureau (:10814) already put security-adjacent
	# rewards on SIA_military_modifier, so this is the established spine, not a new
	# one. New keys added there: terror_threat_detection_modifier,
	# terror_threat_base_defense_modifier, decryption_power, operative_slot (mil);
	# expected_police_modifier, civilian_intel_to_others (politics). intel_network_
	# gain_factor, intelligence_agency_defense, resistance_damage_to_garrison,
	# cyber_capability_modifier, police_cost_multiplier_modifier already existed.
	#
	# Verified-vs-dropped from the spec table: `add_intel` only accepts a country
	# `target`, so "add_intel on the insurgents" (counter_insurgency_south) isn't a
	# valid target - substituted `upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_counter_
	# terror_operations` for the G-category reward instead. `operative_slot` and
	# `civilian_intel_to_others` are modifier keys (set inside a modifier block),
	# not standalone completion_reward effects - wired via the two accumulators
	# above with add_to_variable, same as every other SIA_* reward. Agency icon
	# GFX_intelligence_agency_logo_sia already exists (interface/MD_intelligence_
	# icons.gfx:493, gfx/interface/operatives/agencies/agency_logo_sia.dds on
	# disk) - no bespoke art needed.
	#
	# Deferred events (spec §5e, not wired here): thailand.EX21 (head, agency
	# founding flavour) and thailand.EX22 (SIA_the_watchful_state, surveillance
	# debate) - both left as TODO comments only.
	# Interlocks: counter_insurgency_south gates on has_country_flag = SIA_ds_active
	# (set by SIA_the_southern_question, :6955) and nudges the shared
	# SIA_insurgency_intensity meter via the existing subtract_from_variable/
	# clamp_variable pattern (:6339 etc.) - it does not duplicate the Deep South
	# ladders (:6983+), it reinforces whichever path the player is already on.
	########################

	focus = {
		id = SIA_the_national_intelligence
		icon = central_intelligence_agency

		x = 8
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_ministry_of_defence
		
		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_ministry_of_defence }

		cost = 6

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_LAWS }

		# Matches CZE_bis/UZSI/vojenske_zpravodajstvi precedent (05_czech_republic.txt:10886):
		# bypass with no bypass_effect - if the country already has an agency from
		# another source, this focus completes for free with zero reward.
		bypass = { has_intelligence_agency = yes }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_the_national_intelligence"
			add_ideas = SIA_national_intelligence_idea
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
				create_intelligence_agency = {
					name = "National Intelligence Agency"
					icon = GFX_intelligence_agency_logo_sia
				}
				upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_passive_defense
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_no_lar_dlc_tt
				add_political_power = 50
			}
			# Sacrifice: standing up a national intelligence service is not free.
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 3 }
				change_intelligence_community_opinion = yes
			}
			# TODO: country_event = { id = thailand.EX21 days = 1 } - NIA founding
			# flavour, deferred to the events pass (spec §5e).
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 15
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	## Domestic Policing arm
	focus = {
		id = SIA_special_branch_police
		icon = police_siren

		x = -3
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence

		cost = 5

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_national_intelligence }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_special_branch_police"
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_terror_threat_detection_modifier = 0.08 tooltip = terror_threat_detection_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_terror_threat_base_defense_modifier = 0.06 tooltip = terror_threat_base_defense_modifier_tt }
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_politics_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_pol_police_cost_multiplier_modifier = -0.08 tooltip = police_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
			# Sacrifice: a funded Special Branch raises what citizens expect the
			# police to deliver - a scarce commitment lever (spec §3), not filler.
			add_to_variable = { SIA_pol_expected_police_modifier = 0.25 tooltip = expected_police_modifier_tt }
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = the_military }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 3 }
				change_the_military_opinion = yes
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = SIA_counter_insurgency_south
		icon = generic_counter_insurgency_icon

		x = -1
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_national_intelligence }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_LAWS }

		# Load-bearing: this is the Deep South counter-terror arm, so it requires
		# the shared Deep South crisis to actually be live (set by
		# SIA_the_southern_question, :6955), not just the agency to exist.
		available = {
			has_country_flag = SIA_ds_active
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_counter_insurgency_south"
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_resistance_damage_to_garrison = -0.1 tooltip = resistance_damage_to_garrison_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_terror_threat_base_defense_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = terror_threat_base_defense_modifier_tt }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
				upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_counter_terror_operations
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_no_lar_dlc_tt
				add_political_power = 25
			}
			# Interlock: reinforces whichever Deep South path the player is on,
			# without duplicating the ladders themselves (:6983+).
			custom_effect_tooltip = SIA_insurgency_intensity_decrease_tt
			subtract_from_variable = { SIA_insurgency_intensity = 8 }
			clamp_variable = { var = SIA_insurgency_intensity min = 0 max = 100 }
			# Sacrifice: a heavy hand in the south costs order at home, not a
			# Muslim/ulema faction swing (the_ulema is AA_law_internal_factions.txt's
			# Muslim-internal-faction mechanic, not a guarded interest group Thailand
			# holds - see the B1 header's identical deviation note, :14172).
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_stability_factor = -0.04 tooltip = stability_factor_tt }
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	## Foreign Intelligence arm
	focus = {
		id = SIA_signals_intelligence
		icon = radio_communications

		x = 1
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_national_intelligence }
		allow_branch = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_LAWS FOCUS_FILTER_RESEARCH }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_signals_intelligence"
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_decryption_power = 10 tooltip = decryption_power_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_cyber_capability_modifier = 2 tooltip = cyber_capability_modifier_tt }
			upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_decryption_boost
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_operative_slot = 1 tooltip = operative_slot_tt }
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 3 }
				change_intelligence_community_opinion = yes
			}
			# Sacrifice: a listening station and crypto shop is a standing budget line.
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -12 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 15
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = SIA_humint_networks
		icon = Generic_Intelligence_Gathering

		x = 3
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence

		cost = 5

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_national_intelligence }
		allow_branch = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_LAWS FOCUS_FILTER_DIPLOMACY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_humint_networks"
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_intel_network_gain_factor = 0.06 tooltip = intel_network_gain_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_operative_slot = 1 tooltip = operative_slot_tt }
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = BRM }
				add_intel = {
					target = BRM
					civilian_intel = 10
					army_intel = 5
				}
			}
			# Sacrifice: costly tradecraft is a real political-capital drain.
			add_political_power = -50
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 15
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	## Foreign-partner ME fork (DLC-gated; western vs Chinese tilt)
	focus = {
		id = SIA_cia_partnership
		icon = CIAinvolvement

		x = -1
		y = 2
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_national_intelligence }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = SIA_mss_cooperation }
		allow_branch = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_LAWS FOCUS_FILTER_DIPLOMACY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_cia_partnership"
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = large_increase }
			upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_diplo_training
			# Sacrifice: borrowed capability is borrowed penetration risk too.
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_economy_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_foreign_influence_defense_modifier = -0.04 tooltip = foreign_influence_defense_modifier_tt }
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	focus = {

			add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_foreign_influence_defense_modifier = -0.04 tooltip = foreign_influence_defense_modifier_tt }

		}

	## Capstone spine - needs at least one focus from each arm
	focus = {
		id = SIA_the_watchful_state
		icon = cctv

		x = 0
		y = 3
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence

		cost = 8

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_special_branch_police focus = SIA_counter_insurgency_south }
		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_signals_intelligence focus = SIA_humint_networks }
		allow_branch = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_LAWS }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_the_watchful_state"
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_intel_network_gain_factor = 0.1 tooltip = intel_network_gain_factor_tt }
			upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_anti_partisan
			upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_training_centers
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_operative_slot = 1 tooltip = operative_slot_tt }
			# Sacrifice: the whole apparatus now needs a standing budget.
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -15 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			# TODO: country_event = { id = thailand.EX22 days = 1 } - surveillance
			# debate flavour, deferred to the events pass (spec §5e).
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 15
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	## Capstone ME sacrifice fork - security vs liberty, differ in kind (spec §4b/§6)
	focus = {
		id = SIA_surveillance_state
		icon = big_brother

		x = -1
		y = 4
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_watchful_state }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = SIA_accountable_oversight }
		allow_branch = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_surveillance_state"
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_terror_threat_detection_modifier = 0.15 tooltip = terror_threat_detection_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_intelligence_agency_defense = 0.15 tooltip = intelligence_agency_defense_tt }
			# Sacrifice: maximum detection at a real cost to stability, political
			# capital, and the small/medium business owners who live under it.
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_stability_factor = -0.05 tooltip = stability_factor_tt }
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_politics_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_pol_political_power_factor = -0.05 tooltip = political_power_factor_tt }
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -4 }
				change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = SIA_accountable_oversight
		icon = politics_human_rights

		x = 1
		y = 4
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_national_intelligence

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_watchful_state }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = SIA_surveillance_state }
		allow_branch = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_accountable_oversight"
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_politics_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_pol_political_power_factor = 0.05 tooltip = political_power_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_pol_corruption_cost_factor = -0.08 tooltip = corruption_cost_factor_tt }
			# Sacrifice: a lawful agency is a lower-ceiling agency - real capability
			# traded away, not a free lunch.
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_military_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_mil_terror_threat_detection_modifier = -0.05 tooltip = terror_threat_detection_modifier_tt }
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 2 }
				change_intelligence_community_opinion = yes
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}
}
